	double t_0 = (a1 * a1) * m1;
	double t_1 = (a0 * a0) * m0;
	double tmp;
	if (m2 <= -5e-146) {
		tmp = (3.0 * (((t_0 * w2) / w1) + ((a2 * a2) * m2))) / w2;
	} else if (m2 <= 2e-157) {
		tmp = 3.0 * ((((t_0 / w1) * w0) + t_1) / w0);
	} else {
		tmp = ((3.0 * (a2 * a2)) * (m2 / w2)) - ((-3.0 * t_1) / w0);
	}
	return tmp;
}

                                                              real(8) function code(m0, m1, m2, w0, w1, w2, a0, a1, a2)
use fmin_fmax_functions
    real(8), intent (in) :: m0
    real(8), intent (in) :: m1
    real(8), intent (in) :: m2
    real(8), intent (in) :: w0
    real(8), intent (in) :: w1
    real(8), intent (in) :: w2
    real(8), intent (in) :: a0
    real(8), intent (in) :: a1
    real(8), intent (in) :: a2
    real(8) :: t_0
    real(8) :: t_1
    real(8) :: tmp
    t_0 = (a1 * a1) * m1
    t_1 = (a0 * a0) * m0
    if (m2 <= (-5d-146)) then
        tmp = (3.0d0 * (((t_0 * w2) / w1) + ((a2 * a2) * m2))) / w2
    else if (m2 <= 2d-157) then
        tmp = 3.0d0 * ((((t_0 / w1) * w0) + t_1) / w0)
    else
        tmp = ((3.0d0 * (a2 * a2)) * (m2 / w2)) - (((-3.0d0) * t_1) / w0)
    end if
    code = tmp
end function

                                                              public static double code(double m0, double m1, double m2, double w0, double w1, double w2, double a0, double a1, double a2) {

	double t_1 = (a0 * a0) * m0;
	double tmp;

		tmp = (3.0 * (((t_0 * w2) / w1) + ((a2 * a2) * m2))) / w2;
	} else if (m2 <= 2e-157) {
		tmp = 3.0 * ((((t_0 / w1) * w0) + t_1) / w0);
	} else {
		tmp = ((3.0 * (a2 * a2)) * (m2 / w2)) - ((-3.0 * t_1) / w0);
	}
	return tmp;
}

	t_1 = Float64(Float64(a0 * a0) * m0)

	double t_2 = (a0 * a0) * m0;
	double tmp;

		tmp = (3.0 * (t_0 / w2)) - (-3.0 * (t_2 / w0));

	} else {
		tmp = (3.0 * (((t_1 * w2) / w1) + t_0)) / w2;
	}
	return tmp;
}

                                                                                                                                                                                                              real(8) function code(m0, m1, m2, w0, w1, w2, a0, a1, a2)
use fmin_fmax_functions
    real(8), intent (in) :: m0
    real(8), intent (in) :: m1
    real(8), intent (in) :: m2
    real(8), intent (in) :: w0
    real(8), intent (in) :: w1
    real(8), intent (in) :: w2
    real(8), intent (in) :: a0
    real(8), intent (in) :: a1
    real(8), intent (in) :: a2
    real(8) :: tmp
    if ((0.0d0 - m2) <= (-1d-110)) then
        tmp = 0.0d0 + (m2 * (3.0d0 * (a2 * (a2 / w2))))
    else if ((0.0d0 - m2) <= 4d-63) then
        tmp = 3.0d0 * (((a1 * a1) * (m1 / w1)) + (((a0 * a0) * m0) / w0))
    else
        tmp = (((a2 * a2) * 3.0d0) * m2) / w2
    end if
    code = tmp
end function

	} else if ((0.0 - m2) <= 4e-63) {

	} else {

	}
	return tmp;
}

	return tmp

	end
	return tmp
end

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  double code(double m0, double m1, double m2, double w0, double w1, double w2, double a0, double a1, double a2) {
	double tmp;
	if ((0.0 - m0) <= -1e-93) {
		tmp = 0.0 + (m0 * (3.0 * (a0 * (a0 / w0))));
	} else if ((0.0 - m0) <= -1e-289) {
		tmp = (((a2 * a2) * 3.0) * m2) / w2;
	} else if ((0.0 - m0) <= 1.5e-116) {
		tmp = 0.0 + (((a1 * a1) * (m1 * 3.0)) / w1);
	} else {
		tmp = 3.0 * (((a0 * a0) * m0) / w0);
	}
	return tmp;
}

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  real(8) function code(m0, m1, m2, w0, w1, w2, a0, a1, a2)
use fmin_fmax_functions
    real(8), intent (in) :: m0
    real(8), intent (in) :: m1
    real(8), intent (in) :: m2
    real(8), intent (in) :: w0
    real(8), intent (in) :: w1
    real(8), intent (in) :: w2
    real(8), intent (in) :: a0
    real(8), intent (in) :: a1
    real(8), intent (in) :: a2
    real(8) :: tmp
    if ((0.0d0 - m0) <= (-1d-93)) then
        tmp = 0.0d0 + (m0 * (3.0d0 * (a0 * (a0 / w0))))
    else if ((0.0d0 - m0) <= (-1d-289)) then
        tmp = (((a2 * a2) * 3.0d0) * m2) / w2
    else if ((0.0d0 - m0) <= 1.5d-116) then
        tmp = 0.0d0 + (((a1 * a1) * (m1 * 3.0d0)) / w1)
    else
        tmp = 3.0d0 * (((a0 * a0) * m0) / w0)
    end if
    code = tmp
end function

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  public static double code(double m0, double m1, double m2, double w0, double w1, double w2, double a0, double a1, double a2) {
	double tmp;
	if ((0.0 - m0) <= -1e-93) {
		tmp = 0.0 + (m0 * (3.0 * (a0 * (a0 / w0))));
	} else if ((0.0 - m0) <= -1e-289) {
		tmp = (((a2 * a2) * 3.0) * m2) / w2;
	} else if ((0.0 - m0) <= 1.5e-116) {
		tmp = 0.0 + (((a1 * a1) * (m1 * 3.0)) / w1);
	} else {
		tmp = 3.0 * (((a0 * a0) * m0) / w0);
	}
	return tmp;
}

		tmp = 0.0 + (m0 * (3.0 * (a0 * (a0 / w0))))
	elif (0.0 - m0) <= -1e-289:
		tmp = (((a2 * a2) * 3.0) * m2) / w2
	elif (0.0 - m0) <= 1.5e-116:
		tmp = 0.0 + (((a1 * a1) * (m1 * 3.0)) / w1)
	else:
		tmp = 3.0 * (((a0 * a0) * m0) / w0)
	return tmp

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  function code(m0, m1, m2, w0, w1, w2, a0, a1, a2)
	tmp = 0.0
	if (Float64(0.0 - m0) <= -1e-93)
		tmp = Float64(0.0 + Float64(m0 * Float64(3.0 * Float64(a0 * Float64(a0 / w0)))));
	elseif (Float64(0.0 - m0) <= -1e-289)
		tmp = Float64(Float64(Float64(Float64(a2 * a2) * 3.0) * m2) / w2);
	elseif (Float64(0.0 - m0) <= 1.5e-116)
		tmp = Float64(0.0 + Float64(Float64(Float64(a1 * a1) * Float64(m1 * 3.0)) / w1));
	else
		tmp = Float64(3.0 * Float64(Float64(Float64(a0 * a0) * m0) / w0));
	end
	return tmp
end

end

    else
        tmp = t_0

end function

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              real(8) function code(m0, m1, m2, w0, w1, w2, a0, a1, a2)
use fmin_fmax_functions
    real(8), intent (in) :: m0
    real(8), intent (in) :: m1
    real(8), intent (in) :: m2
    real(8), intent (in) :: w0
    real(8), intent (in) :: w1
    real(8), intent (in) :: w2
    real(8), intent (in) :: a0
    real(8), intent (in) :: a1
    real(8), intent (in) :: a2
    real(8) :: t_0
    real(8) :: t_1
    real(8) :: tmp
    t_0 = (((a1 * a1) * 3.0d0) * m1) / w1
    t_1 = (w1 * (0.0d0 - m1)) * ((-3.0d0) * ((1.0d0 * (a1 / w1)) * (a1 / w1)))
    if (t_1 <= (-1d-104)) then
        tmp = t_0
    else if (t_1 <= 5d-110) then
        tmp = ((a2 * a2) * 3.0d0) * (m2 / w2)
    else
        tmp = t_0
    end if
    code = tmp
end function

	}

end function
